It serves recruiters/hiring managers and the design-engineering community equally: personality on the surface, key information (experience, projects, contact) reachable in seconds.\n8\t\n9\t**Core Value:** The site must feel crafted and memorable (tactile, physics-based motion and strong visual identity) while key info stays instantly accessible, on mobile as much as desktop.\n10\t\n11\t### Constraints\n12\t\n13\t- **Tech stack**: Vite + React + TypeScript + Tailwind — required by motion-primitives and react-spring\n14\t- **Hosting**: GitHub Pages — static output only, needs Actions workflow or a built-output branch\n15\t- **Platform**: Mobile must be excellent — touch interactions, performance on phones\n16\t- **Accessibility/perf**: Assumed baseline: `prefers-reduced-motion` respected, good Lighthouse scores (to confirm with user)\n17\t- **Process**: Detailed documentation before coding; research and surface findings for tooling decisions\n18\t\n19\t<!-- GSD:project-end -->\n20\t\n21\t<!-- GSD:stack-start source:research/STACK.md -->\n22\t\n23\t## Technology Stack\n24\t\n25\t## Bottom Line\n26\t\n27\t## Recommended Stack\n28\t\n29\t### Core Framework\n30\t\n31\t| Technology | Version (verified 2026-10-08) | Purpose | Why | Confidence |\n32\t|------------|-------------------------------|---------|-----|------------|\n33\t| Vite | `^8.3.4` | Build/dev server | Current `latest`. `create-vite` 9.2.1 scaffolds it. That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. Do this only if Lighthouse demands it.\n92\t- Code-split below-the-fold sections with `React.lazy` so the hero ships alone. Any 3D must be lazy-loaded (see Gaps).\n93\t- Not measured: runtime frame rate on a real phone. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Only add if a react-spring-driven drag cannot be done well otherwise. |\n179\t| `vite-plugin-image-optimizer` as the primary image tool | Compress-only (no srcset/format variants). Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). GSAP is now fully free incl. plugins per gsap.com. | Only choose GSAP for pinned/scrubbed multi-step timelines. Not needed for six sections. |\n188\t| **Smooth scroll** | No | **None (native scroll)** | Lenis 1.3.26 (+5.6 kB measured). `lenis/react` provides `ReactLenis`. | Lenis smoothing is **off on touch by default** (`syncTouch: false`), honors reduced motion by default (`respectReducedMotion: true`), but needs `anchors: true` for in-page nav and community reports it can feel unlike native scroll and conflict with focus/keyboard behavior. Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. Default: skip until the design wants it. |\n189\t| **3D / WebGL** | No | **None** (use CSS 3D via Tilt and react-spring transforms for tactility) | (a) OGL 1.0.11 for one hero shader/blob (+12.8 kB measured); (b) three 0.186 + @react-three/fiber 9.8.1 (+241 kB gz measured, peer `react >=19 <19.4`) with `@react-spring/three` | R3F is the one thing in this list that can blow the mobile budget. If 3D is wanted, lazy-load it, cap DPR, and pause off-screen. |\n190\t| **Gesture / drag** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`drag`, `dragConstraints`, `whileTap`, `Reorder`) | motion `drag` | `@use-gesture/react` 10.3.1 only for react-spring-driven drag | `@use-gesture` is stale (Mar 2024). react-spring has no pointer handling itself, so a react-spring drag needs it or hand-written pointer events. |\n191\t| **Page transitions** | **Partly** (`AnimatePresence`; new `AnimateView` in motion 13.4.0 for React 19.3 `ViewTransition`, 2026-09-14, so very new) | **Single page, no router.** Use MorphingDialog/MorphingPopover for project detail | If real routes are wanted: `react-router` (library mode) or `wouter` 3.13 with hash routing + motion `AnimatePresence` | GitHub Pages cannot rewrite SPA URLs. Real routes need a `404.html` copy of `index.html` or hash routing. Note `react-router` 8.4.0 requires Node `>=22.22` and React `>=19.2.7`. `react-router-dom` 7.18.4 still publishes. `AnimateView` is LOW confidence until proven. |\n192\t| **Image optimization** | No | **vite-imagetools 12.0.1** (verified on Vite 8) | Manual pre-resize with `sharp` script, commit outputs | Use `<picture>` with AVIF/WebP + explicit `width`/`height` + `loading=\"lazy\"` below the fold + `fetchpriority=\"high\"` on the hero/LCP image to prevent layout shift. |\n193\t| **Theming / dark mode** | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind `@custom-variant` + inline script (above) | `next-themes` | Covered, see Dark Mode. |\n194\t| **Icons (brand)** | lucide has none | 3-4 inline SVGs for GitHub/LinkedIn/email | `simple-icons` 16.x or `react-icons/si` 5.7 | Lucide for UI glyphs only. |\n195\t| **Physics \"toys\" (stack/pile of objects, rigid-body)** | No | None (react-spring + motion drag can fake most) | matter-js 0.20.0 (last release 2023) or Rapier via `@react-three/rapier` | Only if a falling-objects/gravity playground is part of the identity. Lazy-load. |\n196\t| **Lottie/Rive illustrations** | No | None | `@rive-app/react-canvas` 4.36 (interactive, state machines) | Only if designed assets exist. |\n197\t| **Analytics, forms, contact** | No | `mailto:` / static links | Formspree-style form post | No backend (out of scope). |\n198\t| **Testing** | Not requested | Vitest + Playwright (visual/mobile viewport + axe) | none | Out of the four-tool scope; suggest a mobile-viewport Lighthouse CI check in the deploy workflow. |\n199\t\n200\t## Installation\n201\t\n202\t# Scaffold (Node >=22.12 recommended)\n203\t\n204\t# Core\n205\t\n206\t# Dev\n207\t\n208\t# Then add motion-primitives components one at a time (patch type imports, move to src/)\n209\t\n210\t## Decisions needed from user\n211\t\n212\t## Risks to Carry Into Roadmap\n213\t\n214\t| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |\n215\t|------|----------|------------|\n216\t| motion 14.0.0 is 6 days old and motion releases very frequently (multiple minor versions per month) | Medium | Pin `~14.0.0`, commit lockfile, keep 12.43 / 13.5.1 as fallback versions, avoid `AnimateView` and other brand-new APIs. |\n217\t| motion-primitives is \"beta\", code is copy-in | Low | Treat as owned code. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Lg6NxbUaHrB3Duj8yrjftG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport type Theme = 'light' | 'dark'\n2\t\n3\texport const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'theme'\n4\t\n5\t/** The current theme, read from html[data-theme]. Only 'dark' counts as dark. */\n6\texport function readTheme(): Theme {\n7\t  return document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n8\t}\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * True when the visitor chose a theme but storage refused it. Storage is the record of an\n12\t * explicit choice, so without this flag a blocked-storage visitor would look like one who\n13\t * never chose, and the next OS scheme change would silently undo their choice.\n14\t */\n15\tlet unsavedChoice = false\n16\t\n17\t/**\n18\t * Switch the theme: the attribute is written first so the switch always works, then the\n19\t * choice is stored. When storage is blocked the choice lasts for the page session only, and\n20\t * the OS scheme no longer overrides it.\n21\t */\n22\texport function applyTheme(theme: Theme): void {\n23\t  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n24\t  try {\n25\t    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme)\n26\t    unsavedChoice = false\n27\t  } catch {\n28\t    // Storage blocked (private mode, policy): the in-memory choice still applies.\n29\t    unsavedChoice = true\n30\t  }\n31\t}\n32\t\n33\tconst DARK_QUERY = '(prefers-color-scheme: dark)'\n34\t\n35\tfunction isTheme(value: unknown): value is Theme {\n36\t  return value === 'light' || value === 'dark'\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tfunction storedTheme(): Theme | null {\n40\t  try {\n41\t    const value = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY)\n42\t    return isTheme(value) ? value : null\n43\t  } catch {\n44\t    return null\n45\t  }\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\tfunction systemTheme(): Theme {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    return window.matchMedia(DARK_QUERY).matches ? 'dark' : 'light'\n51\t  } catch {\n52\t    return 'light'\n53\t  }\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\t/** Write the attribute without storing anything, and only when it would change. */\n57\tfunction syncAttribute(theme: Theme): void {\n58\t  if (readTheme() !== theme || !document.documentElement.hasAttribute('data-theme')) {\n59\t    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n60\t  }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t/**\n64\t * Subscribe to theme changes made outside the toggle: another tab changing or clearing the\n65\t * stored choice, and the OS scheme changing while nothing is stored. Both are folded into\n66\t * html[data-theme], which stays the single source of truth, and `onChange` fires when that\n67\t * attribute changes for any reason. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UpaWhBovcNnNTXKTBCGvtj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// Phone-number gate over the built site.\n2\t//\n3\t// Everything in dist is served to the world, and CI logs of a public repository\n4\t// are public too. This fails when a served file holds a phone-shaped string or a\n5\t// tel: link. On a hit it prints only the file and a byte offset (or a PDF page, or a\n6\t// metadata key name), never the matched characters.\n7\t//\n8\t// A PDF is read twice. A byte-level pass over its dictionaries and decoded content streams\n9\t// (below) is kept as a second opinion. The main pass loads it with pdfjs-dist, the parser the\n10\t// browsers' PDF viewers use, and scans the text of every page (joined with spaces and joined\n11\t// with nothing, so a number split across text items still reads as one), every link\n12\t// annotation (any telephone-scheme link fails) and the document metadata (the Info\n13\t// dictionary entry by entry, and the XMP packet). A PDF that cannot be read fails the gate:\n14\t// it exits 0 only after every file and every page has been scanned. dist/resume.pdf must also\n15\t// read as the real resume (the owner's name and at least 1500 non-whitespace characters of\n16\t// text), so the small stand-in file can never be deployed. A dist with no dist/resume.pdf at\n17\t// its root fails too (every Resume link would 404); a resume.pdf elsewhere does not count.\n18\t//\n19\t// Flag: --root DIR (default cwd); scans ROOT/dist.\n20\t// Exit: 0 printing \"no-phone: OK\", 1 on a hit, an unreadable PDF or a missing dist/resume.pdf,\n21\t// 2 when dist is missing.\n22\timport fs from 'node:fs'\n23\timport { createRequire } from 'node:module'\n24\timport path from 'node:path'\n25\timport zlib from 'node:zlib'\n26\t\n27\tconst argv = process.argv.slice(2)\n28\tconst rootFlag = argv.indexOf('--root')\n29\tconst ROOT = path.resolve(rootFlag >= 0 && argv[rootFlag + 1] ? argv[rootFlag + 1] : process.cwd())\n30\tconst DIST = path.join(ROOT, 'dist')\n31\t\n32\tif (!fs.existsSync(DIST) || !fs.statSync(DIST).isDirectory()) {\n33\t  console.error(`no-phone: ${path.relative(ROOT, DIST) || DIST} not found, run the build first`)\n34\t  process.exit(2)\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\tconst SCANNED = /\\.(html|js|css|json|txt|svg|xml|webmanifest|pdf)$/i\n38\t// Markup and text files, where prose can name a number outright. A PDF's decoded text\n39\t// layer reads as prose too, so extracted PDF text is scanned as text (see TEXT_NAME).\n40\t// The PDF's own bytes are not: xref offsets are ten-digit runs.\n41\tconst MARKUP_FILES = /\\.(html|txt)$/i\n42\tconst TEXT_NAME = 'extracted.txt'\n43\tconst PDF_FILES = /\\.pdf$/i\n44\t\n45\t// A tel: link is a URI scheme, so it sits right after a quote, backtick, opening\n46\t// parenthesis, equals sign or tag end (or after whitespace in markup and text). A bare\n47\t// key such as {tel:true} in minified JS, which React DOM ships in its list of input\n48\t// types, is not a link and is not flagged.\n49\t//\n50\t// Separators between the digit groups, in any run: hyphen, dot, slash, space, tab, and the\n51\t// typographic dashes and spaces a word processor or resume produces (en and em dash,\n52\t// non-breaking hyphen, minus sign, no-break, narrow no-break, thin, figure and the other\n53\t// Unicode spaces). Each is matched as the character itself, as its UTF-8 bytes (files are\n54\t// read as latin1, one character per byte), as a JS \\uXXXX escape, and as an HTML entity.\n55\t// A leading +CC or 1 country prefix and a parenthesised area code are allowed.\n56\tconst SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS = [\n57\t  0xa0,\n58\t  0xad,\n59\t  ...Array.from({ length: 0x0b }, (_, i) => 0x2000 + i), // U+2000 to U+200A spaces\n60\t  ...Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => 0x2010 + i), // U+2010 to U+2015 hyphens and dashes\n61\t  0x202f,\n62\t  0x205f,\n63\t  0x2212,\n64\t  0x3000,\n65\t]\n66\tconst hex = (cp, width) => cp.toString(16).padStart(width, '0')\n67\tconst SEPARATOR_FORMS = [\n68\t  '[-. \\\\t/\\\\x96\\\\x97]', // plain ASCII, plus WinAnsi en and em dash in extracted PDF text\n69\t  `[${SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `\\\\u${hex(cp, 4)}`).join('')}]`,\n70\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) =>\n71\t    [...Buffer.from(String.fromCodePoint(cp), 'utf8')].map((b) => `\\\\x${hex(b, 2)}`).join(''),\n72\t  ),\n73\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `\\\\\\\\u${hex(cp, 4)}`),\n74\t  ...SEPARATOR_CODE_POINTS.map((cp) => `&#(?:${cp}|x0*${hex(cp, 1)});`),\n75\t  '&(?:nbsp|ensp|emsp|thinsp|numsp|hyphen|dash|ndash|mdash|horbar|minus);',\n76\t]\n77\tconst SEP = `(?:${SEPARATOR_FORMS.join('|')})`\n78\tconst PREFIX = `(?:\\\\+\\\\d{1,2}${SEP}*|1${SEP}+)?`\n79\t\n80\t// A plausible North American number: area code and exchange start 2 to 9, with an optional\n81\t// 1 or +1 country prefix. Used for runs with no separators at all, where a loose ten-digit\n82\t// pattern would also match hashes and numeric constants. The 32-bit integer limits (and\n83\t// the few values below the signed one) are excluded, since a quoted 2147483647 is a CSS\n84\t// z-index in many libraries.\n85\tconst NANP_RUN =\n86\t  '(?<![0-9A-Za-z])(?!214748364\\\\d|429496729[56])(?:\\\\+?1)?[2-9]\\\\d{2}[2-9]\\\\d{6}(?![0-9A-Za-z])'\n87\t\n88\t// Where a shape applies. `raw` is the file as read (for a PDF, with stream bodies masked\n89\t// out), `literal` is the string literals of a JS or JSON file, `stream` is a decoded PDF\n90\t// content stream and `text` is the text a PDF content stream draws.\n91\tconst LITERAL_FILES = /\\.(js|mjs|cjs|json|webmanifest)$/i\n92\tconst SHAPES = [\n93\t  {\n94\t    label: 'separated number',\n95\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n96\t    re: new RegExp(\n97\t      `(?<!\\\\d)${PREFIX}(?:\\\\(\\\\d{3}\\\\)${SEP}*|\\\\d{3}${SEP}+)\\\\d{3}${SEP}+\\\\d{4}(?!\\\\d)`,\n98\t      'gi',\n99\t    ),\n100\t  },\n101\t  { label: 'tel: link', views: ['raw', 'text', 'stream'], re: /(?<=[\"'`(=>]|^)tel:/gim },\n102\t  { label: 'tel: link', views: ['raw', 'text'], re: /(?<=\\s)tel:/gi, only: MARKUP_FILES },\n103\t  // Standalone digit runs are checked in markup and text only: minified JS legitimately\n104\t  // holds ten-digit constants. A number in a JS string literal is caught by the `literal`\n105\t  // view, which ignores code outside strings.\n106\t  {\n107\t    label: 'ten-digit run',\n108\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n109\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\d{10}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n110\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n111\t  },\n112\t  {\n113\t    label: 'eleven-digit run',\n114\t    views: ['raw', 'text'],\n115\t    re: /(?<![0-9A-Za-z])\\+?1[2-9]\\d{2}[2-9]\\d{6}(?![0-9A-Za-z])/g,\n116\t    only: MARKUP_FILES,\n117\t  },\n118\t  { label: 'unseparated number', views: ['literal'], re: new RegExp(NANP_RUN, 'g') },\n119\t]\n120\t\n121\t// The string literals of a JS or JSON file, kept at their byte offsets with everything else\n122\t// blanked to NUL, so a numeric constant, an identifier or a comment can never match. This is\n123\t// a small tokenizer, not a parser: comments and regex literals are skipped so an apostrophe in\n124\t// either does not shift which quotes open a string, and the `${...}` expressions inside a\n125\t// template literal count as code (minified bundles hold numeric constants there).\n126\tconst REGEX_AFTER = new Set([...'(,=:[!&|?{};+-*%<>~^'])\n127\tfunction literalView(text) {\n128\t  const n = text.length\n129\t  const view = Buffer.alloc(n)\n130\t  const keep = (from, to) => view.write(text.slice(from, to), from, 'latin1')\n131\t  const templateDepth = [] // one entry per open `${`: the `{` nesting inside it\n132\t  let i = 0\n133\t  let prev = '' // last non-space character of code, for telling a regex from a division\n134\t\n135\t  // i is just past an opening backtick or a closing brace of a `${...}`.\n136\t  const templateBody = () => {\n137\t    const from = i\n138\t    while (i < n) {\n139\t      if (text[i] === '\\\\') i += 2\n140\t      else if (text[i] === '`') {\n141\t        keep(from, i)\n142\t        i++\n143\t        return\n144\t      } else if (text[i] === '$' && text[i + 1] === '{') {\n145\t        keep(from, i)\n146\t        i += 2\n147\t        templateDepth.push(0)\n148\t        return\n149\t      } else i++\n150\t    }\n151\t    keep(from, n)\n152\t  }\n153\t\n154\t  while (i < n) {\n155\t    const c = text[i]\n156\t    const next = text[i + 1]\n157\t    if (c === '/' && next === '*') {\n158\t      const end = text.indexOf('*/', i + 2)\n159\t      i = end < 0 ? n : end + 2\n160\t    } else if (c === '/' && next === '/') {\n161\t      const end = text.indexOf('\\n', i)\n162\t      i = end < 0 ? n : end\n163\t    } else if (c === '\"' || c === \"'\") {\n164\t      const from = ++i\n165\t      while (i < n && text[i] !== c && text[i] !== '\\n') i += text[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1\n166\t      keep(from, i)\n167\t      i++\n168\t      prev = c\n169\t    } else if (c === '`') {\n170\t      i++\n171\t      templateBody()\n172\t      prev = c\n173\t    } else if (\n174\t      c === '/' &&\n175\t      (prev === '' ||\n176\t        REGEX_AFTER.has(prev) ||\n177\t        /\\b(?:return|typeof)\\s*$/.test(text.slice(i - 10, i)))\n178\t    ) {\n179\t      let inClass = false\n180\t      for (i++; i < n && text[i] !== '\\n'; i++) {\n181\t        if (text[i] === '\\\\') i++\n182\t        else if (text[i] === '[') inClass = true\n183\t        else if (text[i] === ']') inClass = false\n184\t        else if (text[i] === '/' && !inClass) break\n185\t      }\n186\t      i++\n187\t      prev = '/'\n188\t    } else {\n189\t      if (templateDepth.length > 0) {\n190\t        if (c === '{') templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]++\n191\t        else if (c === '}') {\n192\t          if (templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1] === 0) {\n193\t            templateDepth.pop()\n194\t            i++\n195\t            templateBody()\n196\t            continue\n197\t          }\n198\t          templateDepth[templateDepth.length - 1]--\n199\t        }\n200\t      }\n201\t      if (c > ' ') prev = c\n202\t      i++\n203\t    }\n204\t  }\n205\t  return view.toString('latin1')\n206\t}\n207\t\n208\t// PDF text lives in content streams, usually Flate-compressed, so a raw byte scan of the\n209\t// file cannot see it. Each stream is inflated when it inflates (otherwise its raw bytes are\n210\t// taken as an uncompressed content stream), then the strings it draws (Tj and TJ operators)\n211\t// are pulled out so a number split across kerned runs still reads as one. The number shapes\n212\t// apply to that drawn text only. Stream bytes are never scanned for them: drawing operands\n213\t// are decimals and integers (a move to a three-decimal x and a four-digit y) that read as a\n214\t// separated number, and xref offsets are ten-digit runs. Stream bodies are blanked out of the raw scan for the same\n215\t// reason, so the raw scan still covers the PDF's own dictionaries (document info strings,\n216\t// URI actions) at their byte offsets. tel: links are checked in decoded streams too.\n217\t// Limit: text set in a font with a custom or CID encoding stores glyph ids, not characters,\n218\t// and is not readable without the font's ToUnicode map, and strings inside compressed object\n219\t// streams are not read as text. The resume PDF is also reviewed by hand for that reason.\n220\tfunction pdfStreams(buf) {\n221\t  const text = buf.toString('latin1')\n222\t  const streams = []\n223\t  const open = /stream\\r?\\n/g\n224\t  let m\n225\t  while ((m = open.exec(text)) !== null) {\n226\t    const start = m.index + m[0].length\n227\t    const end = text.indexOf('endstream', start)\n228\t    if (end < 0) break\n229\t    // Resume after \"endstream\", whose tail would otherwise match \"stream\" again.\n230\t    open.lastIndex = end + 'endstream'.length\n231\t    const slice = buf.subarray(start, end)\n232\t    let content = slice\n233\t    try {\n234\t      content = zlib.inflateSync(slice, { finishFlush: zlib.constants.Z_SYNC_FLUSH })\n235\t    } catch {\n236\t      // Not Flate (an image, a font program, plain content): read the bytes as they are.\n237\t    }\n238\t    streams.push({ start, end, content })\n239\t  }\n240\t  return streams\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\t// The PDF text with every stream body replaced by NULs, so offsets are unchanged.\n244\tfunction maskStreams(text, streams) {\n245\t  let out = ''\n246\t  let at = 0\n247\t  for (const { start, end } of streams) {\n248\t    out += text.slice(at, start) + '\\0'.repeat(end - start)\n249\t    at = end\n250\t  }\n251\t  return out + text.slice(at)\n252\t}\n253\t\n254\tfunction decodePdfLiteral(body) {\n255\t  return body.replace(/\\\\(?:([0-7]{1,3})|(\\r?\\n)|(.))/gs, (_all, oct, eol, ch) => {\n256\t    if (oct) return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(oct, 8) & 0xff)\n257\t    if (eol) return ''\n258\t    return { n: '\\n', r: '\\r', t: '\\t', b: '\\b', f: '\\f' }[ch] ?? ch\n259\t  })\n260\t}\n261\t\n262\tfunction decodePdfHex(body) {\n263\t  const hex = body.replace(/\\s+/g, '')\n264\t  const padded = hex.length % 2 ? `${hex}0` : hex\n265\t  const bytes = Buffer.from(padded, 'hex')\n266\t  // A byte order mark marks UTF-16BE text, whose dashes and spaces are real characters.\n267\t  if (bytes[0] === 0xfe && bytes[1] === 0xff) return bytes.subarray(2).swap16().toString('utf16le')\n268\t  // Otherwise dropping NULs reads two-byte text whose characters are plain ASCII.\n269\t  return bytes.toString('latin1').replaceAll('\\0', '')\n270\t}\n271\t\n272\tfunction pdfShownText(content) {\n273\t  const out = []\n274\t  const operand = String.raw`\\((?:\\\\[\\s\\S]|[^()\\\\])*\\)|<[0-9A-Fa-f\\s]*>`\n275\t  const tj = new RegExp(`\\\\[((?:${operand}|[^\\\\]()<])*)\\\\]\\\\s*TJ|(${operand})\\\\s*(?:Tj|'|\")`, 'g')\n276\t  const item = new RegExp(`(${operand})|(-?\\\\d*\\\\.?\\\\d+)`, 'g')\n277\t  const one = (token) =>\n278\t    token.startsWith('(') ? decodePdfLiteral(token.slice(1, -1)) : decodePdfHex(token.slice(1, -1))\n279\t  for (const m of content.matchAll(tj)) {\n280\t    if (m[2] !== undefined) {\n281\t      out.push(one(m[2]))\n282\t      continue\n283\t    }\n284\t    let line = ''\n285\t    for (const part of m[1].matchAll(item)) {\n286\t      if (part[1] !== undefined) line += one(part[1])\n287\t      // A large negative kern in a TJ array is a word gap.\n288\t      else if (Number(part[2]) < -200) line += ' '\n289\t    }\n290\t    out.push(line)\n291\t  }\n292\t  return out.join(' ')\n293\t}\n294\t\n295\tfunction walk(dir, out) {\n296\t  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n297\t    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name)\n298\t    if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(full, out)\n299\t    else if (entry.isFile() && SCANNED.test(entry.name)) out.push(full)\n300\t  }\n301\t}\n302\t\n303\tconst files = []\n304\twalk(DIST, files)\n305\t\n306\tconst hits = []\n307\t// `name` is matched against a shape's `only` filter; `view` selects the shapes that read this\n308\t// text; `label` is what is printed (a file and a byte offset, never the matched characters).\n309\tfunction scan(label, name, view, text) {\n310\t  for (const shape of SHAPES) {\n311\t    if (!shape.views.includes(view)) continue\n312\t    if (shape.only && !shape.only.test(name)) continue\n313\t    for (const match of text.matchAll(shape.re)) {\n314\t      hits.push(`${label} at offset ${match.index} (${shape.label})`)\n315\t    }\n316\t  }\n317\t}\n318\t\n319\t// The shape labels that match somewhere in `text`, each once. A PDF's text has no useful\n320\t// byte offset, so its hits are reported by page or metadata key instead.\n321\tfunction shapeLabels(text) {\n322\t  const labels = new Set()\n323\t  for (const shape of SHAPES) {\n324\t    if (!shape.views.includes('text')) continue\n325\t    if (shape.only && !shape.only.test(TEXT_NAME)) continue\n326\t    shape.re.lastIndex = 0\n327\t    if (shape.re.test(text)) labels.add(shape.label)\n328\t    shape.re.lastIndex = 0\n329\t  }\n330\t  return [...labels]\n331\t}\n332\t\n333\t// pdfjs's own structural flags in `info`; everything else is an entry of the document's Info\n334\t// dictionary, whose key names are listed for the owner to review.\n335\tconst PDFJS_INFO_FLAGS = new Set([\n336\t  'PDFFormatVersion',\n337\t  'Language',\n338\t  'EncryptFilterName',\n339\t  'IsLinearized',\n340\t  'IsAcroFormPresent',\n341\t  'IsXFAPresent',\n342\t  'IsCollectionPresent',\n343\t  'IsSignaturesPresent',\n344\t])\n345\tconst TEL_SCHEME = /^\\s*tel:/i\n346\t\n347\t// The one file visitors are sent to download. The 594-byte stand-in must never ship, so its\n348\t// text layer has to read as a resume: the owner's name and a body of text.\n349\tconst RESUME_FILE = /^dist\\/resume\\.pdf$/\n350\tconst RESUME_NAME = 'Aaron Phan'\n351\tconst RESUME_MIN_CHARS = 1500\n352\t\n353\t// Reads one PDF with pdfjs and records hits. Anything that throws is a hit (fail closed).\n354\tasync function scanPdfWithPdfjs(rel, bytes) {\n355\t  let task\n356\t  const resumeText = []\n357\t  try {\n358\t    const { getDocument } = await import('pdfjs-dist/legacy/build/pdf.mjs')\n359\t    const require = createRequire(import.meta.url)\n360\t    const fontDir = path.join(\n361\t      path.dirname(require.resolve('pdfjs-dist/package.json')),\n362\t      'standard_fonts',\n363\t    )\n364\t    // pdfjs 6.3.289 never evaluates generated code and ignores isEvalSupported (the option was\n365\t    // removed upstream); it stays so a build of pdfjs that honours it is still held to false.\n366\t    /** @type {any} */\n367\t    const params = {\n368\t      data: new Uint8Array(bytes),\n369\t      isEvalSupported: false,\n370\t      useSystemFonts: false,\n371\t      disableFontFace: true,\n372\t      verbosity: 0,\n373\t      standardFontDataUrl: `${fontDir}${path.sep}`,\n374\t    }\n375\t    task = getDocument(params)\n376\t    const doc = await task.promise\n377\t\n378\t    for (let n = 1; n <= doc.numPages; n++) {\n379\t      const page = await doc.getPage(n)\n380\t      const items = (await page.getTextContent()).items.map((item) =>\n381\t        'str' in item ? item.str : '',\n382\t      )\n383\t      resumeText.push(items.join(''))\n384\t      const labels = new Set([...shapeLabels(items.join(' ')), ...shapeLabels(items.join(''))])\n385\t      for (const label of labels) hits.push(`${rel} page ${n} (${label})`)\n386\t      const links = (await page.getAnnotations()).flatMap((a) => [a.url, a.unsafeUrl])\n387\t      if (links.some((url) => typeof url === 'string' && TEL_SCHEME.test(url))) {\n388\t        hits.push(`${rel} (link annotation)`)\n389\t      }\n390\t      page.cleanup()\n391\t    }\n392\t\n393\t    if (RESUME_FILE.test(rel)) {\n394\t      // Whitespace is ignored, so the count does not depend on how the export spaces its words.\n395\t      const compact = resumeText.join('').replace(/\\s+/g, '')\n396\t      if (!compact.includes(RESUME_NAME.replace(/\\s+/g, '')) || compact.length < RESUME_MIN_CHARS) {\n397\t        hits.push(`${rel} is not a real resume (text layer too short or missing the name)`)\n398\t      }\n399\t    }\n400\t\n401\t    const { info = {}, metadata } = await doc.getMetadata()\n402\t    const keys = []\n403\t    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(info)) {\n404\t      if (PDFJS_INFO_FLAGS.has(key)) continue\n405\t      keys.push(key)\n406\t      const text = typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value)\n407\t      if (shapeLabels(text).length > 0) hits.push(`${rel} (metadata ${key})`)\n408\t    }\n409\t    const xmp = typeof metadata?.getRaw === 'function' ? metadata.getRaw() : ''\n410\t    if (xmp) {\n411\t      keys.push('XMP')\n412\t      if (shapeLabels(xmp).length > 0) hits.push(`${rel} (metadata XMP)`)\n413\t    }\n414\t    // Key names only, for review; the values are never printed.\n415\t    console.log(`no-phone: ${rel} metadata keys: ${keys.length > 0 ? keys.join(', ') : 'none'}`)\n416\t  } catch {\n417\t    hits.push(`${rel} could not be read`)\n418\t  } finally {\n419\t    try {\n420\t      await task?.destroy()\n421\t    } catch {\n422\t      // Nothing left to release.\n423\t    }\n424\t  }\n425\t}\n426\t\n427\tfor (const file of files) {\n428\t  const rel = path.relative(ROOT, file).split(path.sep).join('/')\n429\t  const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file)\n430\t  // latin1 keeps one character per byte, so an index is a byte offset.\n431\t  const text = bytes.toString('latin1')\n432\t  if (PDF_FILES.test(file)) {\n433\t    const streams = pdfStreams(bytes)\n434\t    scan(rel, file, 'raw', maskStreams(text, streams))\n435\t    streams.forEach(({ content }, i) => {\n436\t      const decoded = content.toString('latin1')\n437\t      scan(`${rel} (stream ${i + 1})`, file, 'stream', decoded)\n438\t      scan(`${rel} (stream ${i + 1} text)`, TEXT_NAME, 'text', pdfShownText(decoded))\n439\t    })\n440\t    await scanPdfWithPdfjs(rel, bytes)\n441\t    continue\n442\t  }\n443\t  scan(rel, file, 'raw', text)\n444\t  if (LITERAL_FILES.test(file)) scan(rel, file, 'literal', literalView(text))\n445\t}\n446\t\n447\t// The resume must ship at the dist root, where every Resume link points. Only the path is named.\n448\tif (!files.some((file) => RESUME_FILE.test(path.relative(ROOT, file).split(path.sep).join('/')))) {\n449\t  hits.push('dist/resume.pdf is missing (every Resume link would 404)')\n450\t}\n451\t\n452\tif (hits.length > 0) {\n453\t  for (const line of hits) console.error(`no-phone: ${line}`)\n454\t  process.exit(1)\n455\t}\n456\tconsole.log('no-phone: OK')\n457\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01UcpuRVKrK3u9PpYyYHeYzf","agent_id":"a4caeb37f2aa7529f","session_id":"1915a706-941a-4769-90ef-f84e1818906b","uuid":"d3d4d300-c1dc-4330-bee1-cda1bb64d924","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:41:52.698Z","tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01UpaWhBovcNnNTXKTBCGvtj","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"rule"}}],"subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech+arch focus"}

No analytics snippet, tracking tag, form backend or embedded widget was found in `index.html` or `src/`.\n\n## Browser Platform APIs Used\n\nThese are browser features, not external services, but they touch the outside world or persistent state:\n\n- **Clipboard:** `navigator.clipboard.writeText` with an `execCommand('copy')` fallback in `src/lib/copy.ts`. Only the public email address is written. The site never reads the clipboard.\n- **Mail app:** `window.location.href = 'mailto:...'` in `src/lib/copy.ts` (`openMail`) when copying fails.\n- **localStorage:** key `theme` (`THEME_STORAGE_KEY` in `src/lib/theme.ts`). Read by the inline bootstrap script in `index.html` and by `src/lib/theme.ts`. Stores `'light'` or `'dark'`. Writes are wrapped in try/catch for blocked storage.\n- **Cross-tab sync:** `storage` event listener in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`subscribeTheme`).\n- **OS color scheme:** `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')` in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html`.\n- **Reduced motion:** `matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')` in `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, fed to motion's `MotionConfig` and `@react-spring/web` `Globals` by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Viewport/layout observers:** `IntersectionObserver` and `scrollend` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`.\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. Source images live in `src/assets/images/` and are transformed by `vite-imagetools` (`src/content/images.ts`). Steps: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`. Concurrency group `ci-${{ github.ref }}` with cancel-in-progress.\n- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - runs on push to `main` and on `workflow_dispatch`. Three jobs:\n  1. `build`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `configure-pages`, `upload-pages-artifact` (path `./dist`).\n  2. `e2e`: needs `build`. Installs Chromium and runs `npm run test:e2e`.\n  3. `deploy`: needs `build` and `e2e`. Runs `actions/deploy-pages`, then a `curl` smoke check that the live page contains the owner's name.\n- Actions are pinned to full commit SHAs with version comments.\n- Concurrency group `pages`, `cancel-in-progress: false`, so an in-flight deploy finishes.\n\n**Local E2E against a live URL:**\n- Setting `E2E_BASE_URL` makes `playwright.config.ts` skip the local preview server and test that URL.\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None at runtime. The only outbound references are links and browser platform APIs.\n\n**Outbound links (rendered, not fetched):**\n- GitHub profile and a project repository (`https://github.com/...`) - defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` and `src/content/projects.ts` (`links.code` on the RecipeCart entry). The project link opens in a new tab with `rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"` (`src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`).\n- LinkedIn and Instagram profiles - defined in `src/content/contacts.ts` and rendered by `src/sections/Contact/Contact.tsx`.\n- Email - a `mailto:` link built from the `kind: 'email'` entry in `src/content/contacts.ts` (`src/app/Page.tsx` passes it to the sections). The hero's \"Copy email\" control uses `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` (see Browser Platform APIs below).\n\n**Icon and brand assets:**\n- Inline SVG components in `src/components/ui/icons/` (for example `GitHubIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`). No icon CDN or remote font.\n\n**Fonts:**\n- Archivo is self-hosted through `@fontsource-variable/archivo` (imported in `src/main.tsx`). No Google Fonts or other third-party font request.\n\n**Analytics, forms, third-party scripts:**\n- Not detected. No analytics snippet, tracking tag, form backend or embedded widget was found in `index.html` or `src/`.\n\n## Browser Platform APIs Used\n\nThese are browser features, not external services, but they touch the outside world or persistent state:\n\n- **Clipboard:** `navigator.clipboard.writeText` with an `execCommand('copy')` fallback in `src/lib/copy.ts`. Only the public email address is written. The site never reads the clipboard.\n- **Mail app:** `window.location.href = 'mailto:...'` in `src/lib/copy.ts` (`openMail`) when copying fails.\n- **localStorage:** key `theme` (`THEME_STORAGE_KEY` in `src/lib/theme.ts`). Read by the inline bootstrap script in `index.html` and by `src/lib/theme.ts`. Stores `'light'` or `'dark'`. Writes are wrapped in try/catch for blocked storage.\n- **Cross-tab sync:** `storage` event listener in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`subscribeTheme`).\n- **OS color scheme:** `matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')` in `src/lib/theme.ts` and the inline script in `index.html`.\n- **Reduced motion:** `matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')` in `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`, fed to motion's `MotionConfig` and `@react-spring/web` `Globals` by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`.\n- **Viewport/layout observers:** `IntersectionObserver` and `scrollend` in `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`.\n\n## Data Storage\n\n**Databases:**\n- Not applicable. No database client, ORM or connection string is present.\n- All content is static TypeScript data under `src/content/` (`profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `education.ts`, `skills.ts`, `contacts.ts`), typed by `src/content/schema.ts` and exported through `src/content/index.ts` as `siteContent`.\n\n**File Storage:**\n- Local filesystem only, at build time. Source images live in `src/assets/images/` and are transformed by `vite-imagetools` (`src/content/images.ts`). The resume is `public/resume.pdf`, copied to the build output root.\n- Build output: `dist/` (ignored by git, see `.gitignore`).\n\n**Caching:**\n- None at the application level. No service worker. Caching is whatever GitHub Pages applies.\n\n## Authentication & Identity\n\n**Auth Provider:**\n- Not applicable. The site has no user accounts, login or sessions.\n\n**CI credentials (GitHub Actions):**\n- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` deploys through `actions/configure-pages` and `actions/deploy-pages`. The deploy job requests `pages: write` and `id-token: write` (OIDC) only. The build job is read-only (`contents: read`, `pages: read`).\n- No repository secrets are referenced in either workflow.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None. No Sentry, Bugsnag or similar SDK is in `package.json` or `src/`.\n\n**Logs:**\n- Build and gate output goes to the console (`scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`, `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs`). The phone gate deliberately prints file names and offsets only, never matched text.\n- Playwright reporter is `list` (`playwright.config.ts`).\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages, deployed from GitHub Actions (not from a branch). Project site path `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`, set through `VITE_BASE` in both workflows.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - runs on every `push` and `pull_request`. Steps: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`. Concurrency group `ci-${{ github.ref }}` with cancel-in-progress.\n- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - runs on push to `main` and on `workflow_dispatch`. Three jobs:\n  1. `build`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `configure-pages`, `upload-pages-artifact` (path `./dist`).\n  2. `e2e`: needs `build`. Installs Chromium and runs `npm run test:e2e`.\n  3. `deploy`: needs `build` and `e2e`. Runs `actions/deploy-pages`, then a `curl` smoke check that the live page contains the owner's name.\n- Actions are pinned to full commit SHAs with version comments.\n- Concurrency group `pages`, `cancel-in-progress: false`, so an in-flight deploy finishes.\n\n**Local E2E against a live URL:**\n- Setting `E2E_BASE_URL` makes `playwright.config.ts` skip the local preview server and test that URL.\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None at runtime. The site works without any environment variables.\n\n**Build and test variables (non-secret):**\n- `VITE_BASE` - public base path. Read in `vite.config.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`, `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`. Set to `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` in CI. Local default `/`.\n- `E2E_BASE_URL` - optional live target for Playwright (`playwright.config.ts`).\n- `JS_BUDGET_BYTES` - optional override of the 150000-byte gzipped initial JS budget (`scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`).\n- `CI` - read by `playwright.config.ts` (`forbidOnly`, `reuseExistingServer`).\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- The app needs and stores no secrets. `.gitignore` excludes `*.local` and logs; it has no `.env` rule of its own. Do not add credentials to content files: `src/content/` is bundled and served publicly.\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None. No server endpoints.\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None from the app. The deploy job's post-deploy `curl` in `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` is a read-only smoke check against the published Pages URL.\n\n---\n\n*Integration audit: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1563,"cache_read_input_tokens":106163,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1563,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01UcpuRVKrK3u9PpYyYHeYzf","agent_id":"a4caeb37f2aa7529f","session_id":"1915a706-941a-4769-90ef-f84e1818906b","uuid":"0cc8344b-d964-4dd9-88c2-919e6fcf2864","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:43:14.175Z","request_id":"req_011ChDVLYmMwt9DvtM3Pa4mL","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech+arch focus"}

(typed, static, bundled)     │\n│     Image assets `src/assets/images/` via vite-imagetools    │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts React in StrictMode, imports global fonts and CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Wraps `Page` in `Providers` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Reduced-motion context, lazy motion features, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assembles sections in order, derives the email from contacts, runs the one scroll-spy | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver band, click lock, deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Single source of truth is `html[data-theme]`; syncs localStorage, other tabs and OS scheme | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Site header | Phone top bar (below 768 px) and desktop sticky nav (768 px and up) | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` |\n| Phone pill nav | Bottom navigation for phones | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Nav items | Section ids and labels (single list) | `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Projects crate | Horizontal scroll-snap row of sleeves; opens dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Project dialog | Native `<dialog>` with focus restore and prev/next | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time, polite live region | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard with fallback to mailto | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Animation barrel | Public animation API for the rest of the app | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Lazy features | Motion `domMax` bundle loaded after first paint | `src/anim/features.ts`, `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` |\n| Content | Typed static data and the `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Image map | Imagetools imports to `ImageAsset` objects | `src/content/images.ts` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, client-rendered React app with static content and a build-time pipeline. No router, no server, no state library.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `index.html` plus `404.html` (a copy served by GitHub Pages for unknown paths). Navigation is in-page anchors (`#experience`, `#projects`, and so on).\n- Content is data, not components. `src/content/*.ts` holds all copy; sections receive it as props from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Animation is isolated behind a boundary. `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` are the only places allowed to import animation libraries. Enforced by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`, with fixture tests in `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`.\n- Heavy motion code is split out. `motion` features load through a dynamic `import('./features')` in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, so the initial JS stays under the budget checked by `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`.\n- Browser APIs are wrapped in `useSyncExternalStore` hooks (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) rather than local copies of state.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / app shell:**\n- Purpose: Mount the app, provide global context.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: Root component, providers, page composition.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (barrel), `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/content`.\n- Used by: Vite entry (`index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`).\n\n**Layout / chrome:**\n- Purpose: Header, footer, navigation, container and section wrappers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (for `NavIndicator`), `src/components/ui`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections (`Section.tsx`).\n\n**Sections (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section. Pure presentation driven by props.\n- Location: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,Skills,Education,Contact}/`\n- Contains: Section components and their helpers (e.g. `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`).\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section`, `src/components/ui`, `src/anim` (barrel only), `src/hooks`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Must not import content directly. Data arrives as props (oxlint rule in `.oxlintrc.json`: \"Content data may be imported only by src/app/Page.tsx\").\n\n**Components (shared UI):**\n- Purpose: Reusable controls and primitives.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, icon SVGs, `motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored copy-in source).\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib`, `src/hooks`.\n- Used by: Layout, sections, app shell.\n\n**Animation:**\n- Purpose: All motion. Declarative `motion` for mount, layout and gestures; `@react-spring/web` for physics; a single reduced-motion source.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts` (lazy bundle), `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts` (springs, durations, easing), `useMotionPreference.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`. Must not import `sections`, `layout` or `content` (override in `.oxlintrc.json`).\n- Used by: Everything else, through `@/anim` only.\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: Browser-facing logic that is not UI.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`, `theme.ts`)\n- Depends on: DOM APIs only.\n- Used by: Layout, components, app shell.\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The portfolio's data.\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `education.ts`, `skills.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via imagetools in `images.ts`), `schema.ts` types.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html` (`index.html`), which runs the inline script that sets `data-theme` from localStorage or the OS scheme before first paint.\n2. Vite-built module `src/main.tsx` loads the Archivo font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`).\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which wraps `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives `email` from `contacts`, and renders header, sections, footer and phone nav (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `MotionFeatures` schedules `import('./features')` in an idle callback after first paint, unless reduced motion is on (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n\n### Scroll-spy and navigation\n\n1. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once (`src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. The hook observes each section element with an IntersectionObserver whose root margin defines a thin band below the sticky nav.\n3. A click calls `lockTo(id)` from `SiteHeader` or `PillNav`. The indicator is held on the clicked id until `scrollend`, a quiet period, or a 3 s cap.\n4. `activeId` feeds `aria-current` in `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` and the active state in `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`.\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` in state (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`).\n2. `Sleeve` calls `onOpen`, which sets the index.\n3. `ProjectDialog` calls `showModal()` in an effect, focuses the close button and restores focus to the opener on close. Prev and Next wrap around.\n\n### Theme switch\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` writes `html[data-theme]` first, then `localStorage`.\n3. `subscribeTheme` (a MutationObserver on the attribute) re-renders every subscriber. The same function handles `storage` events from other tabs and `prefers-color-scheme` changes while nothing is stored.\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. Local state in components, `useSyncExternalStore` for theme and motion preference, React context for toast (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`) and motion preference (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n- The DOM attribute `data-theme` on `<html>` is the single theme source of truth.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: One responsive image with alt text and AVIF/WebP sources.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (type), `src/content/images.ts` (values).\n- Pattern: Build-time imagetools `?as=picture` imports mapped to a plain object.\n\n**Content records:**\n- Purpose: Typed portfolio data (experience, projects, education, skills, contacts, profile).\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (types), `src/content/projects.ts`, `src/content/experience.ts`.\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly X[]` in each data file, then exported from `src/content/index.ts`.\n\n**Motion tokens:**\n- Purpose: One place for spring configs, durations and easing.\n- Examples: `src/anim/tokens.ts`, re-exported from `src/anim/index.ts` as `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Pattern: Import from `@/anim`, never hard-code spring values in sections.\n\n**Section nav list:**\n- Purpose: Single list of anchored sections, shared by scroll-spy and nav.\n- Examples: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, `NAV_ITEMS`).\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly NavItem[]`.\n\n**Vendored motion primitive:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in source owned by the project, not an npm dependency.\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`.\n- Pattern: Lint rules are switched off for this folder (`.oxlintrc.json` override for `**/motion-primitives/**`).\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML shell:**\n- Location: `index.html` (dev and build entry), `404.html` (second build input, same app).\n- Triggers: Browser navigation; GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: Head metadata, theme bootstrap, `#root` container, a static placeholder shell with section ids (replaced on mount).\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">` in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: `createRoot`, StrictMode, global CSS and font imports.\n\n**Build and test entries:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts` (build), `vitest.config.ts` (unit tests), `playwright.config.ts` (E2E), `scripts/*.mjs` (verify gates).\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run test:e2e`, `npm run verify`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers. The feature bundle is loaded asynchronously, not on a worker.\n- **Global state:** `html[data-theme]` attribute (`src/lib/theme.ts`); module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` set by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`; `featuresStatus` flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`.\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the reviewed files. `src/anim/index.ts` re-exports components that import `@/anim/tokens` directly to avoid the barrel loop.\n- **Import boundaries (enforced by lint):**\n  - Animation libraries (`motion/**`, `@react-spring/**`, `framer-motion`, `react-spring`) only in `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n  - Animation code imported only through `@/anim`, never deep paths.\n  - `src/anim/` must not import `sections`, `layout` or `content`.\n  - Content only in `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- **Static output only:** No server rendering, no runtime fetch. Everything the site shows is in `dist/` at build time.\n- **Base path:** Every asset URL must respect `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or the `VITE_BASE` build value (`vite.config.ts` normalises a trailing slash). Hard-coded absolute paths break the project-site deploy.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing motion libraries in a section or layout file\n\n**What happens:** A component under `src/sections/` or `src/layout/` imports from `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Fails `npm run lint` (oxlint `no-restricted-imports`), and it breaks the bundle split because the lazy motion features are no longer the only consumer boundary.\n**Do this instead:** Use the components and tokens exported by `src/anim/index.ts` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`). Add a new declarative wrapper in `src/anim/declarative/` if none fits.\n\n### Importing content in a section\n\n**What happens:** A section file imports from `@/content` to read its own data.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the lint rule that only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content. It also makes the section impossible to reuse with different data.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop to the section and pass the data from `src/app/Page.tsx` (the pattern used by `Experience`, `Projects`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`).\n\n### Adding a second theme store\n\n**What happens:** A component keeps its own `useState` copy of the theme.\n**Why it's wrong:** The page has more than one theme toggle (phone bar and desktop header), so copies drift. `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` documents this.\n**Do this instead:** Call `useTheme()` from `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`. Write the choice only through `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n\n### Adding a second scroll-spy\n\n**What happens:** A component runs its own IntersectionObserver to pick the active section.\n**Why it's wrong:** Two observers disagree near section boundaries and fight over `aria-current`.\n**Do this instead:** Read `activeId` from the props threaded down by `src/app/Page.tsx`, and call `lockTo` on click.\n\n### Hard-coded asset paths\n\n**What happens:** `href=\"/resume.pdf\"` or `src=\"/src/...\"` in components.\n**Why it's wrong:** The deploy sets `VITE_BASE=/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`, so root-relative paths 404 on GitHub Pages.\n**Do this instead:** Build from `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (as `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` does for `resume.pdf`), or import the asset through Vite.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft in the browser; fail closed in the build gates.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Browser storage access is wrapped in try/catch and falls back to in-memory state (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n- Clipboard write falls back to `execCommand`, then to opening the mail app with a toast (`src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`).\n- A failed motion feature load never settles, so motion components stay static and no unhandled rejection is raised (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n- Build gates exit non-zero on any hit: `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` (exit 1 over budget, 2 missing build), `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` (fails on unreadable PDF or missing `dist/resume.pdf`).\n- No React error boundary was detected in `src/`.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. Console output only in build scripts.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. Content is checked by `satisfies` clauses in `src/content/*.ts` and by unit tests in `tests/unit/content.test.ts` and `tests/unit/content-inventory.test.ts`. No runtime validation of input (there is no input).\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** Skip link (`src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`), `aria-current` on nav, `aria-live` status region in `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, reduced-motion handling in `src/anim/`, axe checks in the test suite.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3257,"cache_read_input_tokens":107726,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3257,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01UcpuRVKrK3u9PpYyYHeYzf","agent_id":"a4caeb37f2aa7529f","session_id":"1915a706-941a-4769-90ef-f84e1818906b","uuid":"a70682da-37b6-46a7-ae93-c0412f43f7c4","timestamp":"2026-11-20T03:43:45.732Z","request_id":"req_011ChDVMH1LwoU1rMs3DJv1c","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech+arch focus"}
