refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                  Static HTML shell (build time)              │\n│   `index.html` (inline theme bootstrap)  `404.html`          │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘\n                            │ <script type=\"module\">\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      Bootstrap                               │\n│   `src/main.tsx`  ->  `src/app/App.tsx`                      │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Providers (app shell)                        │\n│   `src/app/Providers.tsx`                                    │\n│   MotionPreferenceProvider > MotionFeatures > ToastProvider  │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                  Page composition                            │\n│   `src/app/Page.tsx`  (reads `siteContent`, owns scroll-spy) │\n├──────────────┬──────────────────────────┬───────────────────┤\n│  Layout      │   Sections               │  Global chrome    │\n│ `src/layout/`│ `src/sections/*`         │ `PillNav`,        │\n│ SiteHeader,  │ Hero, Experience,        │ `Footer`,         │\n│ Container,   │ Projects, About, Skills, │ ToastProvider     │\n│ Section      │ Education, Contact       │                   │\n└──────────────┴────────────┬─────────────┴───────────────────┘\n                            │ props only\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│     Animation layer `src/anim/` (barrel `@/anim`)            │\n│     Components `src/components/ui/`, `motion-primitives/`    │\n└───────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────┘\n                            ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│     Content data `src/content/` (typed, static, bundled)     │\n│     Image assets `src/assets/images/` via vite-imagetools    │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility | File |\n|-----------|----------------|------|\n| Bootstrap | Mounts React in StrictMode, imports global fonts and CSS | `src/main.tsx` |\n| App root | Wraps `Page` in `Providers` | `src/app/App.tsx` |\n| Providers | Reduced-motion context, lazy motion features, toast context | `src/app/Providers.tsx` |\n| Page | Assembles sections in order, derives the email from contacts, runs the one scroll-spy | `src/app/Page.tsx` |\n| Scroll-spy | IntersectionObserver band, click lock, deep-link jump | `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts` |\n| Theme store | Single source of truth is `html[data-theme]`; syncs localStorage, other tabs and OS scheme | `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` |\n| Site header | Phone top bar (below 768 px) and desktop sticky nav (768 px and up) | `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` |\n| Phone pill nav | Bottom navigation for phones | `src/layout/PillNav.tsx` |\n| Nav items | Section ids and labels (single list) | `src/layout/nav-items.ts` |\n| Projects crate | Horizontal scroll-snap row of sleeves; opens dialog | `src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`, `Sleeve.tsx` |\n| Project dialog | Native `<dialog>` with focus restore and prev/next | `src/sections/Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx` |\n| Toast | One toast at a time, polite live region | `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts` |\n| Copy email | Clipboard with fallback to mailto; used by Hero and Contact | `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`, `src/lib/copy.ts` |\n| Animation barrel | Public animation API for the rest of the app | `src/anim/index.ts` |\n| Lazy features | Motion `domMax` bundle loaded after first paint | `src/anim/features.ts`, `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` |\n| Content | Typed static data and the `siteContent` object | `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts` |\n| Image map | Imagetools imports to `ImageAsset` objects | `src/content/images.ts` |\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, client-rendered React app with static content and a build-time pipeline. No router, no server, no state library.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `index.html` plus `404.html` (a copy served by GitHub Pages for unknown paths). Navigation is in-page anchors (`#experience`, `#projects`, and so on).\n- Content is data, not components. `src/content/*.ts` holds all copy; sections receive it as props from `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Animation is isolated behind a boundary. `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/` are the only places allowed to import animation libraries. Enforced by oxlint `no-restricted-imports` in `.oxlintrc.json`, with fixture tests in `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`.\n- Heavy motion code is split out. `motion` features load through a dynamic `import('./features')` in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`, so the initial JS stays under the budget checked by `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs`.\n- Browser APIs are wrapped in `useSyncExternalStore` hooks (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`, `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts`) rather than local copies of state.\n\n## Layers\n\n**Bootstrap / app shell:**\n- Purpose: Mount the app, provide global context.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: Root component, providers, page composition.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (barrel), `src/components/ui/Toast`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/content`.\n- Used by: Vite entry (`index.html` loads `/src/main.tsx`).\n\n**Layout / chrome:**\n- Purpose: Header, footer, navigation, container and section wrappers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/anim` (for `NavIndicator`), `src/components/ui`, `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections (`Section.tsx`).\n\n**Sections (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One folder per page section. Pure presentation driven by props.\n- Location: `src/sections/{Hero,Experience,Projects,About,Skills,Education,Contact}/`\n- Contains: Section components and their helpers (e.g. `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`).\n- Depends on: `src/layout/Section`, `src/components/ui`, `src/anim` (barrel only), `src/hooks`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Must not import content directly. Data arrives as props (oxlint rule in `.oxlintrc.json`: \"Content data may be imported only by src/app/Page.tsx\").\n\n**Components (shared UI):**\n- Purpose: Reusable controls and primitives.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`, `src/components/motion-primitives/`, `src/components/ui/icons/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx`, `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, icon SVGs, `motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx` (vendored copy-in source).\n- Depends on: `src/anim` barrel, `src/lib`, `src/hooks`.\n- Used by: Layout, sections, app shell.\n\n**Animation:**\n- Purpose: All motion. Declarative `motion` for mount, layout and gestures; `@react-spring/web` for physics; a single reduced-motion source.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `MotionFeatures.tsx`, `features.ts` (lazy bundle), `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts` (springs, durations, easing), `useMotionPreference.ts`, `declarative/` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx`.\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web`. Must not import `sections`, `layout` or `content` (override in `.oxlintrc.json`).\n- Used by: Everything else, through `@/anim` only.\n\n**Hooks and lib:**\n- Purpose: Browser-facing logic that is not UI.\n- Location: `src/hooks/` (`useActiveSection.ts`, `useTheme.ts`), `src/lib/` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `utils.ts`)\n- Depends on: DOM APIs only.\n- Used by: Layout, components, app shell.\n\n**Content:**\n- Purpose: The portfolio's data.\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `education.ts`, `skills.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts`, `index.ts`.\n- Depends on: `src/assets/images/` (via imagetools in `images.ts`), `schema.ts` types.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only.\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (first load)\n\n1. Browser requests `index.html`, which runs the inline script that sets `data-theme` from localStorage or the OS scheme before first paint.\n2. Vite-built module `src/main.tsx` loads the Archivo font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then renders `<App />` into `#root` (`src/main.tsx`).\n3. `App` renders `Providers`, which wraps `Page` (`src/app/App.tsx`, `src/app/Providers.tsx`).\n4. `Page` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives `email` from `contacts`, and renders header, sections, footer and phone nav (`src/app/Page.tsx`).\n5. `MotionFeatures` schedules `import('./features')` in an idle callback after first paint, unless reduced motion is on (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n\n### Scroll-spy and navigation\n\n1. `Page` calls `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` once (`src/app/Page.tsx`, `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`).\n2. The hook observes each section element with an IntersectionObserver whose root margin defines a thin band below the sticky nav.\n3. A click calls `lockTo(id)` from `SiteHeader` or `PillNav`. The indicator is held on the clicked id until `scrollend`, a quiet period, or a 3 s cap.\n4. `activeId` feeds `aria-current` in `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` and the active state in `src/layout/PillNav.tsx`.\n\n### Project detail\n\n1. `Projects` holds `openIndex` in state (`src/sections/Projects/Projects.tsx`).\n2. `Sleeve` calls `onOpen`, which sets the index.\n3. `ProjectDialog` calls `showModal()` in an effect, focuses the close button and restores focus to the opener on close. Prev and Next wrap around.\n\n### Theme switch\n\n1. `ThemeToggle` calls `useTheme().toggle` (`src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts` writes `html[data-theme]` first, then `localStorage`.\n3. `subscribeTheme` (a MutationObserver on the attribute) re-renders every subscriber. The same function handles `storage` events from other tabs and `prefers-color-scheme` changes while nothing is stored.\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. Local state in components, `useSyncExternalStore` for theme and motion preference, React context for toast (`src/components/ui/useToast.ts`) and motion preference (`src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`).\n- The DOM attribute `data-theme` on `<html>` is the single theme source of truth.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**ImageAsset:**\n- Purpose: One responsive image with alt text and AVIF/WebP sources.\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (type), `src/content/images.ts` (values).\n- Pattern: Build-time imagetools `?as=picture` imports mapped to a plain object.\n\n**Content records:**\n- Purpose: Typed portfolio data (experience, projects, education, skills, contacts, profile).\n- Examples: `src/content/schema.ts` (types), `src/content/projects.ts`, `src/content/experience.ts`.\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly X[]` in each data file, then exported from `src/content/index.ts`.\n\n**Motion tokens:**\n- Purpose: One place for spring configs, durations and easing.\n- Examples: `src/anim/tokens.ts`, re-exported from `src/anim/index.ts` as `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`.\n- Pattern: Import from `@/anim`, never hard-code spring values in sections.\n\n**Section nav list:**\n- Purpose: Single list of anchored sections, shared by scroll-spy and nav.\n- Examples: `src/layout/nav-items.ts` (`SECTION_IDS`, `NAV_ITEMS`).\n- Pattern: `as const satisfies readonly NavItem[]`.\n\n**Vendored motion primitive:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in source owned by the project, not an npm dependency.\n- Examples: `src/components/motion-primitives/text-effect.tsx`.\n- Pattern: Lint rules are switched off for this folder (`.oxlintrc.json` override for `**/motion-primitives/**`).\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**HTML shell:**\n- Location: `index.html` (dev and build entry), `404.html` (second build input, same app).\n- Triggers: Browser navigation; GitHub Pages serves `404.html` for unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: Head metadata, theme bootstrap, `#root` container, and a static placeholder shell with section ids that React replaces on mount.\n\n**JS entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`\n- Triggers: `<script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\">` in `index.html`.\n- Responsibilities: `createRoot`, StrictMode, global CSS and font imports.\n\n**Build and test entries:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts` (build), `vitest.config.ts` (unit tests), `playwright.config.ts` (E2E), `scripts/*.mjs` (verify gates).\n- Triggers: `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run test:e2e`, `npm run verify`.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers. The feature bundle is loaded asynchronously, not on a worker.\n- **Global state:** `html[data-theme]` attribute (`src/lib/theme.ts`); module-level `unsavedChoice` flag in `src/lib/theme.ts`; `Globals` from `@react-spring/web` set by `src/anim/MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`; `featuresStatus` flag in `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts`.\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the files reviewed.\n- **Import boundaries (enforced by lint):**\n  - Animation libraries (`motion/**`, `@react-spring/**`, `framer-motion`, `react-spring`) only in `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n  - Animation code imported only through `@/anim`, never deep paths.\n  - `src/anim/` must not import `sections`, `layout` or `content`.\n  - Content only in `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- **Static output only:** No server rendering, no runtime fetch. Everything the site shows is in `dist/` at build time.\n- **Base path:** Every asset URL must respect `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or the `VITE_BASE` build value (`vite.config.ts` normalises a trailing slash). Hard-coded absolute paths break the project-site deploy.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Importing motion libraries in a section or layout file\n\n**What happens:** A component under `src/sections/` or `src/layout/` imports from `motion/react` or `@react-spring/web` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** Fails `npm run lint` (oxlint `no-restricted-imports`), and it bypasses the `src/anim` boundary that keeps the lazy motion bundle the only consumer path.\n**Do this instead:** Use the components and tokens exported by `src/anim/index.ts` (`TextReveal`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`). Add a new declarative wrapper in `src/anim/declarative/` if none fits.\n\n### Importing content in a section\n\n**What happens:** A section file imports from `@/content` to read its own data.\n**Why it's wrong:** Violates the lint rule that only `src/app/Page.tsx` may import content. It also makes the section impossible to reuse with different data.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop to the section and pass the data from `src/app/Page.tsx` (the pattern used by `Experience`, `Projects`, `Skills`, `Education`, `Contact`).\n\n### Adding a second theme store\n\n**What happens:** A component keeps its own `useState` copy of the theme.\n**Why it's wrong:** The page has more than one theme toggle (phone bar and desktop header), so copies drift. `src/hooks/useTheme.ts` documents this.\n**Do this instead:** Call `useTheme()` from `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`. Write the choice only through `applyTheme` in `src/lib/theme.ts`.\n\n### Adding a second scroll-spy\n\n**What happens:** A component runs its own IntersectionObserver to pick the active section.\n**Why it's wrong:** Two observers disagree near section boundaries and fight over `aria-current`.\n**Do this instead:** Read `activeId` from the props threaded down by `src/app/Page.tsx`, and call `lockTo` on click.\n\n### Hard-coded asset paths\n\n**What happens:** `href=\"/resume.pdf\"` or `src=\"/src/...\"` in components.\n**Why it's wrong:** The deploy sets `VITE_BASE=/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/`, so root-relative paths 404 on GitHub Pages.\n**Do this instead:** Build from `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` (as `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx` does for `resume.pdf`), or import the asset through Vite.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Fail soft in the browser; fail closed in the build gates.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- Browser storage access is wrapped in try/catch and falls back to in-memory state (`src/lib/theme.ts`).\n- Clipboard write falls back to `execCommand`, then to opening the mail app with a toast (`src/lib/copy.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`).\n- A failed motion feature load never settles, so motion components stay static and no unhandled rejection is raised (`src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`).\n- Build gates exit non-zero on any hit: `scripts/check-js-budget.mjs` (exit 1 over budget, 2 missing build), `scripts/check-no-phone.mjs` (fails on unreadable PDF or missing `dist/resume.pdf`).\n- No React error boundary was detected in `src/`.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in the app. Console output only in build scripts.\n**Validation:** Compile-time only. `resume.spec.ts`, `not-found.spec.ts`, `motion.spec.ts`.\n- `tests/e2e/mobile/`: phone-only specs (`pill.spec.ts`, `ten-second.spec.ts`).\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, one component per file, file name equals component name (`SiteHeader.tsx`, `ProjectDialog.tsx`).\n- Hooks: `use` prefix, camelCase `.ts` (`useTheme.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `useActiveSection.ts`).\n- Non-component modules: camelCase `.ts` (`theme.ts`, `copy.ts`, `tokens.ts`, `featuresStatus.ts`).\n- Helper modules named for their folder's convention with kebab-case where a file lists names: `nav-items.ts`, `project-types.ts`, `not-found.css`.\n- Content data: lowercase single word matching the collection (`projects.ts`, `experience.ts`).\n- Tests: `*.test.ts` / tooling.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Feature folders under `src/sections/` are PascalCase (`Hero`, `Projects`).\n- Other `src/` folders are lowercase (`app`, `anim`, `lib`, `hooks`, `content`).\n- Kebab-case is used in `src/components/motion-primitives/` (vendored) and `src/anim/declarative/`/`physics/` use lowercase.\n\n**Identifiers:**\n- Section ids are lowercase kebab-case strings and must match the `id` attribute of the section element (`'experience'`, `'skills'`), defined once in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Project ids are kebab-case in `src/content/projects.ts` (`'recipecart'`, `'suspect-genie'`).\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New Section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, wrapped in `Section` from `src/layout/Section.tsx`.\n- Data: add a typed collection and (if it has a nav link) `NAV_ITEMS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for logic, `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts` for the flow.\n\n**New Shared UI Control:**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Hooks for it: `src/components/ui/use<Name>.ts`.\n- Icons: `src/components/ui/icons/<Name>Icon.tsx`.\n\n**New Animation Primitive:**\n- Implementation: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (or `src/anim/physics/` for spring-driven pieces).\n- Export through `src/anim/index.ts` so other folders can use `@/anim`.\n- Tokens: add values to `src/anim/tokens.ts`, not inline in components.\n\n**New Browser Hook or Helper:**\n- Hook: `src/hooks/use<Name>.ts`.\n- Plain helper: `src/lib/<name>.ts`.\n\n**New Content Asset (image):**\n- Source: `src/assets/images/<name>.<ext>`.\n- Import with imagetools query in `src/content/images.ts` (`?w=400;800&format=avif;webp&as=picture`), following the existing entries. Lint rules are switched off here.\n\n**`src/hooks/`:**\n- Purpose: Browser-facing hooks.\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy), `useTheme.ts` (theme store).\n\n**`src/lib/`:**\n- Purpose: Non-React helpers.\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (theme read/write/sync), `copy.ts` (clipboard and mail), `utils.ts` (`cn` class-name helper).\n\n**`src/content/`:**\n- Purpose: All portfolio copy and data.\n- Contains: `schema.ts` (types), `index.ts` (`siteContent`), `profile.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `education.ts`, `skills.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `images.ts`.\n- Key files: `src/content/schema.ts` (add new types here first), `src/content/index.ts` (add new collections here).\n\n**`src/assets/images/`:**\n- Purpose: Source raster images processed by `vite-imagetools`.\n- Contains: `about-dj.jpg`, `capitalone.png`, `spectrum.png`, `symbotic.png`.\n- Generated: Outputs under `dist/assets/` are generated; the sources are committed.\n\n**`src/styles/`:**\n- Purpose: Tailwind v4 entry, design tokens and global CSS.\n- Contains: `index.css` (Tailwind import, `@theme inline` mapping, `@source` paths, `dark` variant), `tokens.css` (palette and theme tokens), `base.css`, `interactions.css` (dialog, band-invert and CTA rules), `not-found.css`.\n- Key files: `src/styles/tokens.css` (palette; `*.spec.ts` under `tests/e2e/`.\n- Build scripts: `check-*.mjs` for gates, `qa-*.mjs` for screenshot tooling.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section folders under `src/sections/` are PascalCase (`Hero`, `Projects`).\n- Other `src/` folders are lowercase (`app`, `anim`, `lib`, `hooks`, `content`, `layout`), and so are the subfolders `declarative/` and `physics/` inside `src/anim/`.\n- `src/components/motion-primitives/` is lowercase and kebab-case because it is vendored source.\n\n**Identifiers:**\n- Section ids are lowercase strings that match the `id` attribute of each section element (`'experience'`, `'skills'`). They are defined once in `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Project ids are kebab-case in `src/content/projects.ts` (`'recipecart'`, `'suspect-genie'`).\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New Section:**\n- Component: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, wrapped in `Section` from `src/layout/Section.tsx`.\n- Data: add a typed collection in `src/content/<name>.ts`, add the type to `src/content/schema.ts`, and export it from `src/content/index.ts`.\n- Wire-up: pass the data as a prop from `src/app/Page.tsx`.
